+            for (index,idx) in t.indexes.iter().filter(|(_,idx)|idx.unique){
+                let mut key=Vec::new();let mut principals=0;
+                for component in &idx.components {
+                    let Some(c)=component.column()else{break;};
+                    let term=if c==&column{RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(role.clone()))}else{match t.columns.get(c).map(C::required){
+                        Some(C::Principal)=>{principals+=1;RuleTerm::Viewer},
+                        Some(C::Ref{table}) if table==target=>RuleTerm::Field("id".into()),
+                        Some(C::Ref{table})=>{
+                            let refs=row.columns.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table:parent} if parent==table)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
+                            if refs.len()!=1{break;}RuleTerm::Field(refs[0].0.clone())
+                        },_=>break,
+                    }};key.push(term);
+                }
+                if principals!=1||key.len()!=idx.components.len(){continue;}
+                let equalities=if idx.components.iter().any(|c|c.column()==Some(&column)){Vec::new()}else{vec![(column.clone(),RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(role.clone())))]};
+                let policy=RuleExpr::Exists{table:roster.clone(),index:index.clone(),key,provenance:snapback4_core::ir::KeyProvenance::Computed,equalities};
+                if !out.contains(&policy){out.push(policy);}break;
+            }
+        }}
+    }out
+}

//! SEC4: intent quality, typed generated populations, and bounded work.
use snapback4_lang::{compile, security::{self,challenges::{self,Campaign,Reachability}}};
fn bound(source:&str)->snapback4_lang::Bound {
    let b=compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);
    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);b
}
const ROLES:&str="use identity password\ntable roles:\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('doctor','volunteer')\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator'\n  online only\n";
#[test]
fn equivalent_intent_is_advice_only_and_semantic_reordering_is_detected(){
    let source="table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  body: text <=80\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\ninvariant notes.read only viewer = .owner and allow\n";
    let b=bound(source);let r=security::analyze(&b);
    assert!(r.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"&&n.site.line==6));
    assert!(!r.refuted());
    let b=bound(&source.replace("viewer = .owner and allow","viewer = .owner or viewer = 'service:operator'"));
    assert!(!security::analyze(&b).notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"));
}
#[test]
fn per_role_population_catches_private_image_and_file_payloads_with_explicit_origin(){
    for field in ["scan: image <=5000", "scan: file <=5000 ('application/pdf')"] {
        let b=bound(&format!("{ROLES}table packages:\n  owner: principal\n  {field}\n  read <- exists roles[viewer]\n  online only\ninvariant packages.scan read only exists roles[viewer, role = 'doctor']\nquery board(c: cursor ?):\n  return packages after c first 100 by .id\n"));
        let mut c=Campaign::default();c.generated_fixture=true;
        let fixture=challenges::generated_fixture(&b,&mut c).unwrap();
        let roles=fixture.store().rows("roles").unwrap();
        for role in ["doctor","volunteer"] {
            assert_eq!(roles.iter().filter(|r|r.field("role").unwrap()==&snapback4_core::Value::String(role.into())).count(),2);
        }
        assert!(fixture.store().rows("packages").unwrap().len()>=2);
        challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&fixture,&mut c);
        let witnesses=c.violations.iter().filter(|v|v.reachability==Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        assert!(!witnesses.is_empty(),"{c:?}");
        assert!(witnesses.iter().any(|v|v.viewer.contains("volunteer")&&v.operation=="board"));
        let mut r=security::analyze(&b);challenges::apply(&mut r,&c);assert!(r.refuted());
        // A later synthetic witness must not replace the generated fixture provenance.
        let supplemental=challenges::run(&b);challenges::apply(&mut r,&supplemental);
        assert_eq!(r.invariants[0].counterexample.as_ref().unwrap().reachability,Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace);
    }
}
#[test]
fn generated_memberships_refer_to_valid_resource_and_distinct_owners(){
    let b=bound("table teams:\n  title: text 1..80\n  read <- allow\n  online only\ntable members:\n  team: teams\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('admin','member')\n  unique team, principal\n  read <- allow\n  online only\n");
    let mut c=Campaign::default();let fixture=challenges::generated_fixture(&b,&mut c).unwrap();
    let teams=fixture.store().rows("teams").unwrap();let members=fixture.store().rows("members").unwrap();
    assert!(teams.len()>=2&&members.len()>=2,"{c:?}");
    assert_ne!(members[0].field("principal"),members[1].field("principal"));
    assert!(members.iter().all(|m|teams.iter().any(|t|t.field("id")==m.field("team"))));
}

#[test]
fn check_exhaustion_is_explicit_and_retains_no_proof_assumptions(){
    let b=bound("table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  body: text <=80\n  read <- allow\n  online only\ninvariant notes.body read only .owner = viewer\nquery notes():\n  return notes first 100 by .id\n");
    let r=security::analyze(&b);assert!(r.invariants.iter().all(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved));
    let c=challenges::run_selected_after(&b,None,Campaign::bounded(0));

#[test]
fn board_advice_names_the_unscoped_branch_but_not_resource_scoped_roles(){
    let source=format!("{ROLES}table reports:\n  owner: principal\n  rating: enum('good','bad')\n  scan: image <=5000 ?\n  read <- .owner = viewer or (exists roles[viewer, role = 'volunteer'] and .rating = 'bad')\n  online only\n");
    let b=bound(&source);assert!(security::analyze(&b).notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"));
    let b=bound(&source.replace(" and .rating = 'bad'"," and .owner = viewer and .rating = 'bad'"));
    assert!(!security::analyze(&b).notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"));
}
#[test]
fn dominating_argument_guard_suppresses_excluded_role_and_names_actual_risk(){
    let source=format!("{ROLES}table docs:\n  read <- exists roles[viewer, role = 'doctor']\n  online only\nmutation register(role: enum('doctor','volunteer')):\n  require role = 'volunteer' else NOT_ALLOWED\n  return insert roles {{principal: viewer,role}}\n").replace("insert <- viewer = 'service:operator'","insert <- .principal = viewer");
    let b=bound(&source);let mut r=security::analyze(&b);security::add_source_notes(&mut r,&[("schema.q".into(),source.clone())],&b);
    assert!(!r.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION"),"{:?}",r.notes);
    let source=source.replace("  require role = 'volunteer' else NOT_ALLOWED\n","");
    let b=bound(&source);let mut r=security::analyze(&b);security::add_source_notes(&mut r,&[("schema.q".into(),source)],&b);
    assert!(r.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION"&&n.message.contains("'doctor'")),"{:?}",r.notes);
}

}

#[test]
fn draft_candidates_are_never_equivalent_to_the_read_gate(){
    let b=bound(&format!("{ROLES}table docs:\n  body: text <=80\n  read <- exists roles[viewer, role = 'doctor']\n  online only\n"));
    let draft=security::draft(&b);
    assert!(!draft.lines().any(|line|line.contains("invariant docs.body")&&line.contains("'doctor'")));
    assert!(draft.lines().any(|line|line.contains("invariant docs.body")&&line.contains("'volunteer'")));
}

#[test]
fn rejected_fallback_final_exists_world_never_becomes_a_failing_fixture(){
    let source="table roots:\n  creator: principal\n  read <- allow\n  after insert <- exists members[root = .id]\n  online only\ntable members:\n  root: roots\n  proof: image <=1\n  unique root\n  read <- allow\n  online only\ninvariant roots.read only deny\nquery roots():\n  return roots first 100 by .id\n";
    let b=bound(source);let mut c=Campaign::default();

    challenges::generated_campaign(&b,&mut c,false);
    assert!(c.violations.is_empty(),"{c:?}");

}

#[test]
fn bounded_campaign_charges_initial_worlds_and_every_mutation_step(){
    let b=bound("table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  body: text <=80\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\nmutation add():\n  return insert notes {owner: viewer,body: 'x'}\ninvariant notes.read only .owner = viewer\nquery inspect():\n  return notes first 100 by .id\n");
    let mut c=Campaign::bounded(10000);c.work_remaining=Some(1);
    let fixture=challenges::generated_fixture(&b,&mut c).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(c.work_remaining,Some(0));
    challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&fixture,&mut c);
    assert_eq!(c.attempted,0);assert!(c.unsupported.iter().any(|s|s.contains("work budget exhausted")));
    let fixture=snapback4_core::Engine::memory(b.schema.clone(),b.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone())).unwrap();
    let mut c=Campaign::bounded(10000);c.work_remaining=Some(1);
    challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&fixture,&mut c);
    assert_eq!(c.attempted,0,"the one reservation pays for the replay world, leaving no mutation step");
}

fn validate_fixture(bound:&Bound,engine:&mut Engine)->Result<(),String>{
    let actual=readback(engine)?;
    for (name,values) in &actual {for row in values {
        snapback4_core::engine::validate::validate_row(&bound.schema,name,row).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        for c in &bound.schema.tables[name].constraints {if let Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table,index,key}=c {
            let rule=RuleExpr::Exists{table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),key:key.clone(),provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed,equalities:Vec::new()};
            if engine.fixture_policy(&rule,snapback4_core::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL,None,row,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=rules::Verdict::Readable {return Err(format!("{name}: trusted seed did not establish final-exists constraint"));}
        }}
    }}
    Ok(())
}

fn replay(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,word:&str,row:&Value)->Result<(bool,Value),String>{
    let name="$security.probe";let table=inv.table.clone();let key=id(&table);
    let read=Read{site:"security challenge".into(),table:table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),key:vec![Expr::Literal(key.clone())],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed};
    let mut seeds=rows.clone();
    let probe=match word{
        "read"=>program(name,ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(read))]),
        "insert"=>{seeds.remove(&table);program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Insert{table,row:Expr::Literal(row.clone())},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])},
        "delete"=>program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Delete{table,key:Expr::Literal(key)},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]),
        "update"=>{
            let definition=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
            let changed=definition.columns.iter().filter(|(n,_)|n.as_str()!="id"&&!definition.engine_field(n))
                .filter(|(n,_)|inv.column.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|c==*n))
                .filter(|(n,_)|!definition.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(n))))
                .find_map(|(n,k)| {let old=row.field(n).ok()?;let next=match (k.required(),old){
                    (C::Bool,Value::Bool(v))=>Value::Bool(!v),
                    (C::Enum{variants},_)=>Value::String(variants.iter().find(|v|Some(Value::String((*v).clone()))!=Some(old.clone()))?.clone()),
                    (C::Text{max},Value::String(v)) if v.chars().count()<*max as usize=>Value::String(format!("{v}x")),
                    (C::Int|C::Time,Value::Int(v))=>Value::Int(v+1),_=>return None};
                    Some((n.clone(),next))});
            let Some((column,value))=changed else{return Err("no valid changed mutable-column sample".into());};
            program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Update{table,key:Expr::Literal(key),patch:Expr::Literal(Value::Map([(column,value)].into_iter().collect()))},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])
        },_=>return Err("row integrity/transition challenge is not an operation admission test".into()),
    };
    let mut engine=engine(bound,&seeds,probe)?;
    if word=="read"{return engine.query(name,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map(|q|(q.data!=Value::Null,row.clone())).map_err(|e|e.to_string());}
    let outcome=engine.mutate(name,None,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    if !matches!(outcome.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..}){return Ok((false,row.clone()));}
    let next=engine.store().rows(&inv.table).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.into_iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
    if word=="delete"{return if next.is_none(){Ok((true,row.clone()))}else{Err("delete returned sent without removing the target".into())};}
    let next=next.ok_or("sent write has no committed target image")?;
    if word=="update" {
        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
        let changed=table.columns.keys().filter(|c|!table.engine_field(c)&&!table.maintained.contains_key(*c))
            .any(|c|row.field(c).ok()!=next.field(c).ok()&&inv.column.as_ref().is_none_or(|column|column==c));
        if !changed{return Err("sent no-op/implicit-only update has no changed declared target column".into());}
    }
    Ok((true,next))
}
fn readback(engine:&Engine)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{engine.schema().tables.keys().map(|t|engine.store().rows(t).map(|rs|(t.clone(),rs)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).collect()}

fn operation_preflight(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<(),String>{
    let Some(name)=&inv.operation else{return Ok(());};
    let p=&bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==name).ok_or("operation did not bind")?.program;
    if !super::census::body(&p.body){return Err(format!("{name}: opaque/loop/scan path requires write-boundary tracing"));}
    let rule=inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("operation policy outside rule fragment")?;
    if rule.any_term(&|t|!matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))){return Err("operation policy requires write-boundary row-image tracing".into());}
    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
    if writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).count()>1{return Err(format!("{name}: multiple target writes need executed-effect tracing; net images do not identify delete/reinsert"));}
    let unstable=kernel::dependencies(rule).iter().any(|dep|{
        let hits=writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==dep).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        !hits.is_empty()&&!(dep==&inv.table&&hits.len()==1&&hits[0].1=="insert")
    });
    if unstable||!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty()||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.sequences.is_empty()||!t.maintained.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())){
        return Err("operation policy dependency may change before/after its write boundary; snapshot correspondence unproved".into());
    }Ok(())
}

/// Schema-only engine challenges. Successful synthetic operations refute the
/// declared table-authority contract, not application-operation reachability.
pub fn run(bound:&Bound)->Campaign{run_selected(bound,None)}
/// `check` searches unproved direct obligations; unsearched blockers remain
/// explicitly unproved. Default `test` passes None and runs the whole set.
pub fn run_selected(bound:&Bound,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>)->Campaign{run_selected_after(bound,sites,Campaign::default())}
/// Continue after trusted observations, preserving one set of work budgets.
pub fn run_selected_after(bound:&Bound,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>,mut out:Campaign)->Campaign{
    // A direct component theorem needs no repeated concrete search for that
    // same component. Indirect observers, temporal reads and actual authored
    // operations below still run; the whole declaration remains unproved.
    let discharged=super::analyze(bound).invariants.into_iter()
        .filter(|v|v.direct_rule==Status::Proved&&v.invariant.operation.is_none()
            && (matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"|"insert"|"delete")
                || matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"update"|"write")&&bound.schema.tables[&v.invariant.table].written_by.is_empty()))
        .map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut assertions=bound.invariants.clone();
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue;}
        if inv.operation.is_some()||matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"){continue;}
        for p in &bound.programs {if p.program.kind!=ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}
            let relevant=ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(table,kind)|table==&inv.table&&(inv.target=="write"||inv.target==*kind||*kind=="upsert"));
            if relevant{let mut op=inv.clone();op.operation=Some(p.program.name.clone());assertions.push(op);}
        }
    }
    for inv in &assertions{
        if out.exhausted(){break;}
        if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.reachability.is_reachable()){continue;}
        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue;}
        if sites.is_some_and(|sites|!sites.contains(&inv.site.to_string())){continue;}
        if inv.operation.is_none()&&discharged.contains(&inv.site.to_string()){continue;}
        if let Err(why)=operation_preflight(bound,inv){out.unsupported.insert(why);continue;}
        let mut found=false;let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
        let combinations=bound.schema.tables.values().flat_map(|t|t.columns.values()).filter_map(|k|if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){Some(variants.len())}else{None}).fold(1usize,usize::saturating_mul).max(24);

+/// Only exact kernel implication results are reused, never concrete judgments.

+    let key=serde_json::to_string(&(admission,policy)).unwrap();

//! Advice identifies a risk shape; it never supplies a proof assumption.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#123-actionable-authority-advice — current roles, grant writers, required evidence and refusal oracles.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramKind};
const NOW_FOR_DEFAULTS:i64=1_800_000_000_000;
fn rule_parts(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<&RuleExpr>{match rule{RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().flat_map(rule_parts).collect(),RuleExpr::Not(x)=>rule_parts(x),r=>vec![r]}}
fn role_test(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {rule_parts(rule).iter().any(|r|match r{
    RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{
        if super::privilege::membership_probe(bound,r,table){return true;}
        let columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
        super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,t)|columns.contains(c)&&matches!(t,RuleTerm::Literal(_)))
    },_=>false,
})}
fn reader_columns(rule:&RuleExpr)->BTreeSet<String>{rule_parts(rule).into_iter().filter_map(|r|match r{RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(f))=>Some(f.clone()),_=>None}).collect()}
fn note(notes:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>,site:&Site,code:&str,message:String,rewrite:&str){notes.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,message,rewrite).guide("security"));}
fn mutable_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->bool {
    let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(name)else{return false;};
    let mut roles=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name);
    if roles.is_empty()&&let Some(principal)=super::privilege::global_principal(bound,name){roles.insert(principal);}
    if roles.is_empty(){return false;}
    let mutable=roles.iter().any(|c|!t.constraints.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(c))));
    fn supplied(value:&serde_json::Value,roles:&BTreeSet<String>)->bool{
        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Object").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Literal").and_then(|v|v.get("Map").or_else(||v.get("Row"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
        true // Dynamic patches cannot establish that role fields are untouched.
    }
    fn writes(value:&serde_json::Value,name:&str,roles:&BTreeSet<String>,update:bool,delete:bool)->bool{
        match value{
            serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>{
                for tag in ["Update","Upsert","WriteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
                    let target=if tag=="WriteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
                    if update&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name)&&body.get(if tag=="Upsert"{"row"}else{"patch"}).is_some_and(|v|supplied(v,roles)){return true;}
                }}
                for tag in ["Delete","DeleteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
                    let target=if tag=="DeleteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
                    if delete&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name){return true;}
                }}
                xs.values().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete))
            },serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete)),_=>false,
        }
    }
    bound.programs.iter().any(|p|{
        let system=p.program.kind==snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job;
        let update=mutable&&(system||!matches!(t.rules.update,RuleExpr::Deny)||roles.iter().any(|c|t.written_by.contains_key(c)));
        let delete=system||!matches!(t.rules.delete,RuleExpr::Deny);
        writes(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),name,&roles,update,delete)
    })
}
fn recipient_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str,branch:&RuleExpr,grant:Option<&str>,roster:&str,effect:&str)->bool{
    if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==*name&&(i.target==effect||i.target=="write"&&matches!(effect,"insert"|"update"))&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::dependencies(p).contains(roster)&&role_test(bound,p)&&kernel::implies(branch,p)!=Ok(true))){return true;}
    let _=(branch,grant,roster);
    // Field names cannot establish a current-role contract. Without independent
    // intent, retained access may be deliberate, so lifetime advice stays silent.
    false
}
pub(super) fn notes(bound:&Bound,exposures:&[Exposure])->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#131-intent-and-diagnostics — equivalence is advice, never refusal.
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        if inv.operation.is_some()||inv.target=="always" {continue;}
        let Some(policy)=&inv.policy else{continue;};
        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
        let restates=super::effects(inv).iter().all(|word|{
            if matches!(*word,"insert"|"update")&&!table.written_by.is_empty(){return false;}
            let gate=if *word=="read"{super::columns::admission(table,inv.column.as_deref())}else if *word=="update"{super::images::stable(table,&table.update_rule_judged())}else{table.rules.for_effect(super::effect(word)).clone()};
            let intent=if *word=="update"{super::images::stable(table,&policy.old_and_next())}else{policy.clone()};
            kernel::implies(&gate,&intent)==Ok(true)&&kernel::implies(&intent,&gate)==Ok(true)
        });
        if restates {note(&mut notes,&inv.site,"N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE",format!("{}: this invariant restates the rule and can't catch a mistake in it; state the spec's intent instead",inv.target_label()),"rewrite from the spec: who may see/do what, for which resource and lifecycle state? Declare sensitive-column intent and current-role/relationship conditions independently; review security-invariants.q.draft");}
    }
    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        if t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable(){note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by every signed-in user: {}",t.rules.read.quarry()),"confirm the spec permits these columns always, or only while a lifecycle condition holds, and declare read intent; snapback4 audit lists inherited grants and their writers");}
        if let RuleExpr::Exists{table:roster,key,equalities,..}=&t.rules.read
            && equalities.is_empty()&&key.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer)) {
            note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by any signed-in user with a matching {roster} row; no particular role or row ownership is required"),"confirm that every roster member should see all these columns; declare intent from the privacy spec and inspect audit");
        }
        // A role-wide board branch returns the entire row, including private payload.
        if t.columns.values().any(|kind|kind.contains_asset())||bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==*name&&i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_some()) {
            for branch in t.rules.read.disjuncts() {
                let parts=rule_parts(branch);
                let role=parts.iter().any(|part|role_test(bound,part));
                let row_filter=parts.iter().any(|part|matches!(part,RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(_),RuleTerm::Literal(_))|RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Field(_),RuleTerm::Literal(_))|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(_),RuleTerm::Field(_))|RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Literal(_),RuleTerm::Field(_))));
                let unscoped=parts.iter().all(|part|match part{
                    RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}=>key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
                    _=>!part.has_reference(),
                })&&reader_columns(branch).is_empty();
                if role&&row_filter&&unscoped {note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ",format!("{name}.read: role-wide branch `{}` returns private or media columns for every matching row",branch.quarry()),"publish a board/projection table containing only intended public fields; protect private columns with independent column-read intent");break;}
            }
        }
        for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
            if kind.contains_asset() && (kind.is_optional() || matches!(kind.required(),C::List{..}|C::ListRange{min:0,..})) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE",format!("{name}.{column} permits omitted or empty media evidence"),"if the spec requires evidence, declare a required image/file or [image] 1..N; otherwise retain optionality deliberately (guide security)");
            }
        }
        for leaf in rule_parts(&t.rules.insert) {
            if let RuleExpr::Created {table:parent,..}=leaf
                && bound.schema.table(parent).is_some_and(|p| p.rules.insert.disjuncts().iter().any(|r| matches!(r,RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(_),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(_)))))
                && bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other| [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r| rule_parts(r).iter().any(|r|matches!(r,RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if table==name&&matches!(key.as_slice(),[RuleTerm::Viewer])&&equalities.is_empty())))) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_VACUOUS_RULE",format!("{name}: `created {parent}` can bootstrap authority trusted globally by a viewer-only probe; a caller may create their own global administrator grant"),"provision global authority only through an operator/service seat or existing authorized admin; group creation must scope every later grant to that group (guide deploy, first-admin setup)");
            }
        }
        for (effect,rule) in [("read",&t.rules.read),("insert",&t.rules.insert),("update",&t.rules.update),("delete",&t.rules.delete)] {
          for branch in rule.disjuncts(){
            for dep in kernel::dependencies(branch){if let Some(g)=bound.schema.table(&dep) {
                let rosters=[&g.rules.insert,&g.rules.update].into_iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).filter(|r|r!=&dep&&mutable_role(bound,r)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
                if rosters.iter().any(|r|recipient_role(bound,name,branch,Some(&dep),r,effect))&&(role_test(bound,&g.rules.insert)||role_test(bound,&g.rules.update)){
                    note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{effect}: `{}` trusts retained {dep} grants while current role in {} can change",branch.quarry(),rosters.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),"recheck the current role in this branch or revoke every derived grant transactionally on role changes; guide security");
                }
            }}
            let declared=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==*name&&(i.target==effect||i.target=="write"&&matches!(effect,"insert"|"update"))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).flat_map(kernel::dependencies).filter(|r|mutable_role(bound,r)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
            if !reader_columns(branch).is_empty()&&declared.iter().any(|r|recipient_role(bound,name,branch,None,r,effect)) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{effect}: principal branch `{}` does not recheck the declared mutable current role",branch.quarry()),"recheck the current role as well as principal attribution; guide security");
            }
          }
        }
    }
    // Inspect original syntax for argument flow and distinct authored codes.
    // Keeping lints outside Bound.notes preserves the compiler's existing
    // diagnostic contract; check/audit expose this security advice separately.
    let _=exposures;
    let mut retained=BTreeSet::new();
    notes.retain(|n|n.code!="N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE"||retained.insert(n.message.split(':').next().unwrap_or("").to_string()));
    notes
}
pub(super) fn source_notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    let documents=sources.iter().map(|(file,source)|crate::parse_document(file,source).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let catalog=super::super::catalog::Catalog::build(documents.clone(),&mut Vec::new());
    for doc in documents{
        for p in &doc.programs {
            let mut body=p.prelude.clone();body.extend(p.body.clone());
            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
            let mut guarded=BTreeSet::new();
            for statement in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body) {
                if let Stmt::Require{condition,..}=statement {
                    let mut condition=condition.clone();
                    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut condition,&mut |e|if let Expr::Exists{read,..}=e {
                        for key in &read.keys {if let Expr::Name{name,..}=&key.value {guarded.insert(name.clone());}}
                    });
                }
            }
            for (write,context) in super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p){
                let Expr::Write{op,table,fields,site,..}=&write else{continue;};
                let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table)else{continue;};
                let role_columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
                let principal=super::privilege::global_principal(bound,table);
                let reassigns=principal.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==c)&&!t.constraints.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(c))));
                if role_columns.is_empty()&&p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert)||*op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&reassigns){
                    let operation=super::super::operation_name(std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy().as_ref(),&p.name);
                    let requirements=catalog.tables.get(table).map(|info|super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,&context)).unwrap_or_default();
                    if super::privilege::membership_grant_risk(bound,table,&operation,&requirements,if *op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert&&!reassigns{ast::WriteOp::Insert}else{*op},fields)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),"$membership".into())){
                        note(&mut out,site,"N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION",format!("{}: {table} membership grants authority, but assignment does not establish the independent provisioning intent",p.name),"restrict membership grants to the declared provisioner; use guide staging's first-admin recipe");
                    }
                }
                for column in &role_columns {if p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column)||matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert)&&t.defaults.contains_key(column))&&bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other|[&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(table))) {
                    let mut targets=fields.iter().filter(|f|&f.name==column).filter_map(|f|match &f.value{Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(s),..})=>Some(s.clone()),_=>None}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                    if !fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column)&&let Some(snapback4_core::Value::String(role))=t.defaults.get(column).map(|d|d.value(NOW_FOR_DEFAULTS,"$advisory")){targets.push(role);}
                    let chosen=fields.iter().find(|f|&f.name==column);
                    let dynamic=chosen.map_or(targets.is_empty(),|f|!matches!(f.value,Some(Expr::Literal{..})));
                    if let Some(field)=chosen {
                        if let Some(Expr::Name{name:argument,..})=&field.value&&let Some(arg)=p.args.iter().find(|a|a.name==*argument)&&let ast::TypeKind::Enum{variants}= &arg.type_.kind {targets=variants.clone();}
                    }
                    // Restrict the actual assigned argument by dominating literal guards.
                    // Never narrow a literal assignment using a same-named unrelated argument.
                    if let Some(Expr::Name{name:argument,..})=chosen.and_then(|f|f.value.as_ref()) {
                        if targets.is_empty(){targets=super::privilege::role_values(bound,table,column);}
                        targets.retain(|target|context.body.iter().filter_map(|s|if let Stmt::Require{condition,..}=s{Some(condition)}else{None}).all(|condition|argument_guard(condition,argument,target)!=Some(false)));
                        if targets.is_empty(){continue;}
                    }
                    let requirements=catalog.tables.get(table).map(|info|super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,&context)).unwrap_or_default();
                    let effective_op=if chosen.is_none()&&*op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{ast::WriteOp::Insert}else{*op};
                    if let Some(risk)=super::privilege::risk(bound,table,column,&super::super::operation_name(std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy().as_ref(),&p.name),&targets,effective_op,&requirements,dynamic,fields)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),format!("$privilege:{column}"))) {
                        note(&mut out,site,"N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION",format!("{}: {risk}; review the grant's delegation policy",p.name),"restrict role assignment to authorized delegation or an operator seat; use guide staging's first-admin recipe (intentional open enrollment may permit this)");
                    }
                }}
            }
            super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e|{
                let Expr::Write{table,fields,site,..}=e else{return};
                let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table)else{return};
                let readers=reader_columns(&t.rules.read);
                for f in fields.iter(){
                    let arg=f.value.as_ref().and_then(|e|if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e{Some(name.as_str())}else{None}).unwrap_or(f.name.as_str());
                    if !guarded.contains(arg)&&readers.contains(&f.name)&&p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==arg)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),f.name.clone())){
                        note(&mut out,site,"N_ARGUMENT_GRANT",format!("{} writes argument {arg} straight into reader-grant column {table}.{}",p.name,f.name),"require the chosen principal's current role and relationship before granting access; caller authority alone does not validate the recipient");
                    }

                }
            });
            // Distinct absent and denial codes disclose logical existence to
            // callers admitted by a broader table rule, even without payload.
            fn refusals(body:&[Stmt],bound:&Bound,absent:&mut Option<String>,codes:&mut Vec<(Site,String)>){for s in body{match s{
                Stmt::Bind{value:Expr::Assert{value,code,..},..}=>{
                    *absent=None;
                    if let Expr::Read(read)=value.as_ref()&&let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(&read.table){
                        let own=read.keys.iter().any(|k|matches!(&k.value,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"));
                        if !t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable()&&!own {
                            // A table read gate already conflates hidden and absent.
                            // Only broader program-level privacy intent can make
                            // its later refusal distinguish private existence.
                            if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==read.table&&i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|policy|kernel::implies(&t.rules.read,policy)==Ok(false))){*absent=Some(code.clone());}
                        }
                    }
                },
                Stmt::Require{site,code,..}=>if let Some(a)=absent.as_ref()&&a!=code{codes.push((site.clone(),format!("lookup absence {a}, later denial {code}")));},
                Stmt::If{then_body,else_body,..}=>{refusals(then_body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes);refusals(else_body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes);},
                Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>refusals(body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes),_=>{}}}}
            let mut codes=Vec::new();refusals(&p.body,bound,&mut None,&mut codes);
            for (site,why) in codes.into_iter().take(1){note(&mut out,&site,"N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE",format!("{}: {why}; row readership is broader than declared private-existence intent",p.name),"use the same absence/denial code and message for private existence, or tighten the row audience");}
            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Job {
                fn guards(body:&[Stmt],out:&mut Vec<Expr>){for s in body {match s{
                    Stmt::Require{condition,..}=>out.push(condition.clone()),
                    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>{out.push(condition.clone());guards(then_body,out);guards(else_body,out);},
                    Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>guards(body,out),_=>{}
                }}}
                let mut job_guards=Vec::new();guards(&p.body,&mut job_guards);
                let clock=job_guards.iter().any(|g|{let mut g=g.clone();let mut found=false;super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut g,&mut |e|if matches!(e,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"){found=true;});found});
                if !clock{continue;}
                let mut writes=BTreeSet::new();let mut b=p.body.clone();
                super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut b,&mut |e|if let Expr::Write{table,fields,..}=e&&fields.iter().any(|f|f.name=="status"){writes.insert(table.clone());});
                for table in writes {for m in doc.programs.iter().filter(|m|m.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation){
                    let mut aliases=BTreeSet::new();
                    for s in &m.body{if let Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=s{
                        let v=if let Expr::Assert{value,..}=value{value.as_ref()}else{value};
                        if matches!(v,Expr::Read(r) if r.table==table){aliases.extend(names.clone());}
                    }}
                    let mut checks=Vec::new();guards(&m.body,&mut checks);let(mut status,mut now)=(false,false);
                    for mut c in checks{super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut c,&mut |e|match e{
                        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"=>now=true,
                        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="status"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Name{name,..} if aliases.contains(name))=>status=true,_=>{}
                    });}
                    let module = std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("schema");
                    let job_name = if module == "schema" { p.name.clone() } else { format!("{module}.{}", p.name) };
                    let already_named = bound.notes.iter().any(|n| n.code == "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" && n.site == m.site
                        && n.message.contains(&format!("column {table}.status)"))
                        && n.message.contains(&format!("scheduled job {job_name} ")));
                    if status&&!now&&!already_named{note(&mut out,&m.site,"N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE",format!("{} gates on {table}.status while clock job {} changes that status; writes can run between the deadline and sweep",m.name,p.name),"enforce the deadline against server now in the mutation too; a sweep cleans up state but does not enforce an exact deadline");}
                }}
            }
        }
    }
    out
}

fn argument_guard(expr:&Expr,argument:&str,target:&str)->Option<bool>{
    match expr {
        Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..} if op=="="||op=="!="=>{
            let pair=match (left.as_ref(),right.as_ref()){
                (Expr::Name{name,..},Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(value),..})|(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(value),..},Expr::Name{name,..}) if name==argument=>Some(value),_=>None,
            }?;Some((pair==target)==(op=="="))
        },
        Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..} if op=="and"||op=="or"=>{
            let (a,b)=(argument_guard(left,argument,target),argument_guard(right,argument,target));
            if op=="and"{if a==Some(false)||b==Some(false){Some(false)}else if a==Some(true)&&b==Some(true){Some(true)}else{None}}
            else if a==Some(true)||b==Some(true){Some(true)}else if a==Some(false)&&b==Some(false){Some(false)}else{None}
        },Expr::Unary{op,value,..} if op=="not"=>argument_guard(value,argument,target).map(|v|!v),_=>None,
    }
}

 succeeded in 0ms:
//! Paired logical observations, replayed independently in memory.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — two valid worlds and repeatable observations are required.
use super::*;

#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Observations {
    pub present: serde_json::Value,
    pub absent: serde_json::Value,
    pub absent_rows: BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,
}

pub(super) fn observe(engine:&mut Engine,p:&Program,viewer:&str,args:BTreeMap<String,Value>)->Result<serde_json::Value,String>{
    let context=Context::new(viewer,args).at(NOW);
    if p.kind==ProgramKind::Query {
        match engine.query(&p.name,context) {
            Ok(q) if q.unavailable || !q.complete => Err("incomplete/metered query is outside logical payload comparison".into()),
            Ok(q)=>Ok(serde_json::json!({"data":q.data})),
            Err(e) if e.rule.is_some() && matches!(e.family.as_str(),"rule"|"constraint") => Ok(serde_json::json!({"refusal":{"code":e.code,"message":e.message}})),
            Err(e)=>Err(format!("query error is not an admitted observation: {e}")),
        }
    }else{
        match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,context).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.state {
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{result,..}=>Ok(serde_json::json!({"sent":result})),
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{why,current}=>{
                // E_RULE can also mask authorization-budget exhaustion. Only
                // an authored require/constraint carries this rule marker;
                // bound/storage/schema/ambiguous engine refusals cannot be a
                // logical counterexample in our resource-excluding contract.
                if why.rule.is_none()||!matches!(why.family.as_str(),"rule"|"constraint") {
                    return Err(format!("resource/engine or ambiguous mutation refusal {} is outside paired logical comparison",why.code));
                }
                Ok(serde_json::json!({"refusal":{"code":why.code,"message":why.message},"current":current}))
            },
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c|c=="id")&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))) {
        if inv.policy.is_none(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired observation needs complete bound policy dependencies; unsupported policy AST is not dependency-free",inv.table));continue;}
        if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){continue;}
        // Closed transitive observer set: include maintained targets, inherited
        // readers and every table whose rule probes an affected population.
        // An operation mentioning none of them has no logical input changed
        // by this pair. This prunes experiments, never proves an obligation.
        let mut observers=BTreeSet::from([inv.table.clone()]);
        loop {
            let before=observers.len();
            for m in &bound.schema.maintains {if observers.contains(&m.source){
                observers.insert(m.target.clone());
                if let Some(owner)=&m.private{observers.insert(owner.table.clone());}
            }}
            for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables {
                if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|observers.contains(&p.parent))
                    || [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|d|observers.contains(d))) {
                    observers.insert(name.clone());
                }
            }
            if observers.len()==before{break;}
        }
        // Removing an ordinary row must not manufacture an invalid reference
        // graph. More general paired deletions need a checked closure.
        if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))) {
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired absence needs a referentially valid world closure",inv.table));continue;
        }
        for variant in 0..8 {
            if !out.reserve(1){return;}
            let Ok(present)=snapshot(bound,inv,variant)else{continue;};
            if present.keys().any(|name|bound.schema.tables[name].columns.values().any(C::contains_asset)) {
                out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired fixture asset identities are not coupled",inv.table));continue;
            }
            let Some(row)=present.get(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first()).cloned()else{continue;};
            let prepared=(||->Result<Value,String>{
                let e=engine(bound,&present,program("$security.pair.image",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))?;
                let actual=readback(&e)?;
                actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).cloned().ok_or_else(||"protected paired row is absent after validation".into())
            })();
            let row=prepared.as_ref().unwrap_or(&row);
            let mut absent=present.clone();absent.remove(&inv.table);
            // Final-exists validation is a pure judgment of this fixed seeded
            // world, before operation/viewer admission. Retain its rejection
            // across observers, never cache execution/resource/policy results.
            let invalid=std::cell::RefCell::new([None::<String>,None]);
            for viewer in [VIEWER,OTHER,"account:security-no-role","service:security"] {
                for bp in &bound.programs {
                    let p=&bp.program;
                    if !matches!(p.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.native {continue;}
                    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
                    let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                    if writes.iter().any(|(table,kind)|table==&inv.table||deps.contains(table)||*kind!="update")
                        || !writes.is_empty() && (!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty() || bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.sequences.is_empty()||!t.on_delete.is_empty())) {
                        out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired mutation lacks stable policy/implicit-write and deterministic-id reduction",p.name));continue;
                    }
                    let json=serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap();
                    if !observers.iter().any(|table|super::super::audit::mentions(&json,table)){continue;}
                    // Fresh generated ids and opaque viewer/work contexts are
                    // not repeatable functions of these paired inputs.
                    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool {match v{
                        serde_json::Value::String(s)=>s=="NewId"||s=="JobAs",
                        serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"Emit"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"))||m.values().any(opaque),
                        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false,
                    }}
                    if opaque(&json){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque effects excluded from paired determinism",p.name));continue;}
                    let cases=histories::fixture_cases(p,&present,&[VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into()],out);
                    if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
                    for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !out.reserve(12){return;}
                        if out.paired_observer_calls+4>8192{out.unsupported.insert("paired observers capped at 8192 call reservations; remaining worlds not covered".into());return;}
                    out.paired_observer_calls+=4;
                    let execute=|seeds:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>|->Result<(serde_json::Value,BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>),String>{
                        if let Err(why)=&prepared{return Err(why.clone());}

                        let slot=usize::from(std::ptr::eq(seeds,&absent));
                        if let Some(why)=&invalid.borrow()[slot]{return Err(why.clone());}
                        let mut e=match engine(bound,seeds,program("$security.pair",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){
                            Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{
                                if why.ends_with(": trusted seed did not establish final-exists constraint"){invalid.borrow_mut()[slot]=Some(why.clone());}
                                return Err(why);
                            },
                        };
                        let actual=readback(&e)?;
                        // Judge the hypothetical protected row in each complete
                        // committed world, before any operation can change roles.
                        let focal=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||row.clone());
                        if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,&focal,&mut e)!=Ok(false){return Err("paired policy is not definitely false in both worlds".into());}
                        observe(&mut e,p,viewer,args.clone()).map(|value|(value,actual))
                    };
                    let ((a,rows),(b,absent_rows))=match (execute(&present),execute(&absent)) {
                        (Ok(a),Ok(b))=>(a,b),
                        (Err(e),_)|(_,Err(e))=>{
                            if !e.starts_with("paired policy is not definitely false") {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.name));}
                            continue;
                        },
                    };
                    out.attempted+=2;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=2;
                    if a==b {continue;}
                    // The query order is engine-defined. Recreate both worlds
                    // to decline store-scoped cursors or unstable outputs.
                    if !matches!((execute(&present),execute(&absent)),(Ok((aa,_)),Ok((bb,_))) if aa==a&&bb==b){
                        out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired observation is not repeatable",p.name));continue;
                    }
                    out.violations.push(Counterexample {reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:args.clone(),viewer:viewer.into(),
                        operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"read observation".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:rows,
                        rule_path:"paired present/absent engine executions differ while the declared privacy policy is false in both".into(),
                        scope:"schema-valid seed-ingress paired worlds; authenticated logical data/refusal only; no application reachability claim".into(),
                        observations:Some(Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows})});
                    break;
                    }
                    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.observations.is_some()){break;}
                }
                if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.observations.is_some()){break;}
            }
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.observations.is_some()){break;}
        }
    }
    out.unsupported.insert("paired observations exclude wire sequences/receipts, physical timing and resource/meter differences; HTTP and device histories require separate qualification".into());
}

# Security invariants

State privacy intent separately from enforcement. Check reports proved, refuted
with a replayed counterexample, or unproved with a reason; passing finite tests
are not proofs.

```quarry
table privateNotes:
  patient: principal
  body: text 1..2000
  sealed: bool default false
  read <- .patient = viewer and .sealed = false
  insert <- .patient = viewer and .sealed = false
  online only
-- Spec: a patient may see only their own note; sealing may narrow availability.
-- Removing an accidental broad branch from the rule must not change this intent.
invariant privateNotes.read only .patient = viewer
invariant privateNotes.body read only .patient = viewer
invariant privateNotes.insert only .patient = viewer
query privateNote(note: privateNotes):
  return privateNotes[note]
mutation writePrivateNote(body: text 1..2000):
  return insert privateNotes { patient: viewer, body }
```

Run `snapback4 check` → `snapback4 test` → `snapback4 audit`. Check reports
invariant counts and five actionable notes; `--verbose` retains every reason.
A mirror invariant cannot catch a wrong rule: the advisory asks
for the spec's audience, resource relationship and lifecycle condition instead.
A legitimate simple owner policy can be equivalent; the advisory never refuses it.
Review `snapback/generated/security-invariants.q.draft`: it offers unconfirmed
per-role/per-column alternatives and questions, never auto-accepted policy.

Test synthesizes two users per discovered role/seat and related ownership rows
without fixture files, including password-only apps. These private in-memory
fixtures never open dev doors. Every failure labels its initial fixture origin:
`generated-fixture-trace` means an authored observation from a validated generated
initial state, not proof that app operations created that state. Author-supplied
fixtures retain `authored-fixture-trace`. Both fail acceptance on actual observed
violations; seed-only/synthetic table probes remain non-failing supplemental evidence.
Fixture-free tests report that no authored business cases ran. Finite passing
campaigns never prove security. Check bounds concrete exploration to 512 call/world
reservations and 1500 ms; exhaustion stays unproved and points to the full test.
`audit --json` retains all column audiences, grant writers, exclusions and uncovered
role/row/effect exposures. Declaring one effect does not cover the others.

## Reference

`invariant table.column read only P` protects a column value, derived values and value-dependent counts/existence/refusals. An authored projection excluding the column does not by itself disclose that value; filtering, ordering or copying it can. Audit JSON includes `column_audiences` (effective row rule plus private-owner gate). Unknown derived and opaque paths remain unproved.

`invariant table.read only P` protects read intent; insert, update and delete
name authority. `invariant table.column write only P` covers writes to a
column (both insert and update). `invariant table.column update only P` checks
only edits, retaining old-and-next-image semantics; use it when creation is permitted
to a different role (for example counter staff creating a ticket price). `invariant table always P` states row integrity.
An update policy over `.field` constrains **both old and next images**.
A policy saying status must not be override therefore also restricts editing
a row that was already override, even when changing it away from that status.
For the role authority of one named mutation, use operation-specific intent
(`invariant table.operation only P`) instead. General next-image-only and
cross-operation transition assertions still need a separate reduction.
Predicates use the existing rule vocabulary: `.field`, `viewer`, `exists`,
comparisons, `and`, `or`, `not`. For a role write use the complete roster probe,
for example `invariant members.role write only exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']`
in a schema declaring those tables. A declaration never grants authority. Whole-row read intent also protects logical
existence: when occupancy totals are intentionally public but roster identities
are private, declare column read intent for those identities instead. Include
a service seat explicitly when the spec permits it; seats are ordinary checked
viewers, never implicitly exempt.

The implemented proof fragment exhaustively checks independent three-valued
rule predicates. A failed implication is unproved until an engine replay
establishes a counterexample. A schema-valid seeded snapshot is not a claim
that an application operation sequence reaches that state. Required-column
non-null assertions have a separate validation theorem. Read/count/sum/existence obligations are reported separately. A maintained aggregate with bare privacy keys can prove its contributor audience; a broader aggregate can yield a paired present/absent counterexample. Exact server `not before`/`expire at` bounds can prove a temporal read component independently of physical sweeps. General transitions and unsupported expressions remain unproved.
Read-through captures/live parents, complex derived aggregates, raw rule probes,
owned-field update bypasses, cascade/set-null and opaque jobs/workers need
separate reductions. An unresolved path blocks the whole declaration's proof.

Server delivery authorizes against the current server snapshot. A disconnected
device can retain an earlier snapshot until it receives a revocation; even
`until-revoked` cannot know a later server role. Synced current-role intent is
therefore unproved for device access. Retained history follows its leave policy.
These reports exclude physical timing and resource/meter side channels and
explicitly exclude the trusted system-principal boundary. Service seats remain
inside the ordinary-viewer check; there is no implicit seat exemption.

## Current roles and derived grants

A membership or assignment created while a user is an inspector can survive
their demotion. Conjoin the **current reader's role** with the assignment
branch, or revoke every derived grant transactionally whenever the role changes.
A grant writer's role does not establish the recipient's current authority.
With independent current-role intent, grant-lifetime advice points at this pattern; the argument-grant advice identifies
arguments written into reader-grant columns: validate the chosen principal's
role and relationship, even when the caller is privileged.
Global admin authority must come from a trusted operator or existing admin;
a group-creation `created` probe must remain scoped to that group everywhere.
`N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION` identifies role assignments trusted by later rules without independent delegation. Open enrollment may be intentional: compare each note with the specification. See `guide staging` for operator first-admin setup and `guide sharing` for parent authority.

## Required evidence and deadlines

If the spec requires a photo, use a required `image <=5000000` column or
`[image <=5000000] 1..4`; `?` permits omission and `[image] <=4` permits zero.
Uploads are verified media and asset reads inherit row and private-field rules.
Optional-evidence advice asks the builder to check intentional optionality.
Text `min..max` counts non-whitespace Unicode characters toward the minimum;
the maximum counts all characters. Stored text is not trimmed or rewritten.
A sweep changes state eventually: compare the deadline with server `now` in
the mutation too if writes must stop exactly at that instant.

## Existence observations

A lookup that says NOT_FOUND on absence followed by a denial saying FORBIDDEN
can reveal existence to callers admitted by a broader read rule. Use the same
code **and message** for hidden/absent/denied cases when existence is private,
or tighten the table read rule. Program `exists` is caller-filtered; probes
inside table rules inspect raw rows and can disclose predicates indirectly.
Inspect both paths in `audit`; a payload-only read check is insufficient.

## When an observer remains unproved

The report names the edge that lacks a reduction. Preserve the specification
while changing its enforcement; weakening an invariant to match a leaking
rule does not repair privacy.

- Maintained counts/sums: retain privacy fields as ordinary bare group keys
  and protect the aggregate with the same audience predicate. Recorded
  reference paths, calendar keys, derived values, private columns and temporal
  contributors need their own reduction; their names are not source fields.
- Owner fields: declare column-write intent too. `written by` authorizes
  patches even when the ordinary update rule denies. To prove a particular
  mutation's role authority, use a dominating current-role requirement and
  operation-specific intent over a role table the mutation cannot change.
  This does not prove every owner-field or old/next-image path.
- Raw rule probes: inspect the observing table and its writers. A protected
  target's read rule does not protect predicates probed from another rule.
  Apply the intended audience at that observer, or remove the raw dependency;
  the report retains the unsupported edge until it can establish implication.
- Cascade/set-null: automatic child changes bypass child write rules. An
  explicit authorized child mutation with `restrict` removes that automatic
  writer, but parent refusal/existence and transitive references remain
  separate observations. Read-through/live-parent authority requires a separate reduction.
- Jobs/native workers/actions: the audit names the trusted or opaque writer.
  An ordinary mutation with stable role requirements can prove that mutation's
  authority; arbitrary native/worker output cannot acquire a proof from a
  caller's role or a passing finite test.
- Schedules: use a stored `not before`/`expire at` lifetime for exact read
  boundaries, or compare server `now` in the write itself. A scheduled status
  update leaves the old status visible between deadline and sweep.

Default `test` challenges pure-schema applications through a private production
`serve`: unauthenticated calls, forged persona headers and development doors.
The host uses no pre-existing developer credentials or development mode. Applications needing
native/worker artifacts are explicitly not covered by that schema-only host.
Focused fixtures also enable short ordered mutation histories followed by
ordinary reads; traces record actual calls and current policy evaluation.
Named personas/service seats and enum argument combinations are enumerated
within reported limits. Asset challenges use real foreign-upload and verified
non-image ledgers; video arguments and unvisited histories remain named. Complete fixture stores preserve image/file ledgers, captures, read-through deadlines, maintained views and expiry. Histories include fixture principals and reference-row candidates, under a reported work cap. Neither
these finite campaigns nor an offline device's retained snapshot establishes
current-server revocation at a disconnected access.

### E_SECURITY_TARGET

E_SECURITY_TARGET means the invariant target does not name a declared table, column or effect. Correct the target name; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

### N_SECURITY_INTENT

N_SECURITY_INTENT means access rules lack independent declared intent. Declare policy from the spec; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

### N_BROAD_READ

N_BROAD_READ identifies universal or roster-wide readership; its message states the exact condition. Confirm the intended audience; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

### N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE

N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE means a stored grant may survive a later role change. Recheck the current reader role; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

### N_ARGUMENT_GRANT

N_ARGUMENT_GRANT means an argument becomes a reader-grant column. Validate the recipient role and relationship; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

### N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE

N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE means optional media permits omitted evidence. Declare required media when the spec requires it; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

### N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE

N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE means sweeps leave time between a deadline and cleanup. Enforce the deadline in the mutation; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

### N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE

N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE means absence and later denial can reveal different outcomes. Unify the code and message when existence is private; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

The isolated production HTTP campaign also signs in, refreshes the session,
logs out all sessions and retries both retired credentials against query,
mutation, schema, changes, sync, probe and refresh doors. It reports missing coverage
when ordinary identity or complete deployed worker artifacts are unavailable.
This checks server admission; a disconnected device can still retain old bytes.

### N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION

N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION identifies self-signup roles or role-grant operations that can confer authority trusted by other rules. Restrict assignment to authorized delegation; provision the first administrator with `guide staging`.

Identity rate refusals return HTTP `Retry-After` (seconds) and
`denied.retryAfterMs` (milliseconds), conservatively covering the 60-second
window. Wait that interval before retrying identity requests.

REL5 owns release transitions/old-client compatibility; MIG owns backfills and
staging clock control for day-two qualification.

### N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE

N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE means intent and the effective rule admit the same cases
in the bounded predicate kernel. Check the original requirement before rewriting; an equivalent policy
may be correct, but cannot detect a mistake copied into both declarations.

### N_ROLE_WIDE_READ

N_ROLE_WIDE_READ means a role-wide branch gated by a row value exposes every column
on matching rows.
Use a board/projection table for a public rating; declare intent for the private
notes, principals, images and files separately.

//! A campaign fork retains activated semantics without running adoption again.
use super::*;
impl Engine {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — isolation includes entropy, assets and ephemeral ownership.
    pub fn fork_fixture(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        if self.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.ephemeral.is_some()) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs an isolated ephemeral-hub snapshot".into()));
        }
        if self.presence.is_some(){return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs an isolated external presence snapshot".into()));}
        let store=self.store.fork_memory()?;
        let identity=store.meta("store_id")?.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("fixture store has no identity".into()))?;
        Ok(Self {
            timeline_schedule:timeline::Schedule::default(),
            ephemeral:std::sync::Arc::new(ephemeral::Hub::new(identity,self.generation,&self.schema)),
            store,schema:self.schema.clone(),programs:self.programs.clone(),ceilings:self.ceilings,
            entropy:crate::ids::seed(),minter:crate::ids::Mint::default(),generation:self.generation,
            expiry_after:self.expiry_after.clone(),dev_effects:self.dev_effects,
            #[cfg(feature="native")] native:self.native.clone(),
            #[cfg(feature="native")] native_logs:native::Logs::default(),presence:None,
        })
    }
}

impl Engine {
    /// Judge independent fixture intent with the engine's authorized rule hops.
    pub fn fixture_policy(&mut self,rule:&crate::schema::RuleExpr,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,next:&Value,now:i64)->Result<rules::Verdict,RuntimeError>{
        let tx=self.store.read_transaction()?;
        let context=if viewer==crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL{Context::system()}else{Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new())};
        let mut runner=Runner::new(&tx,&self.schema,context.at(now),Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(),Cut::Abort),0);
        let bytes=runner.rule_scratch(rule);
        runner.authorized_scratch(bytes,&[],|runner,allowance|{
            let mut probe=read::RuleProbe{runner,allowance};
            rules::evaluate_verdict(rule,viewer,old,next,false,&mut probe)
        })
    }
}
